Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the enumerative space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 453 and the convergence rate is O(324/n^4).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to enumerative structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 453/324 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The enumerative operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 222. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 492 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to enumerative theory, we decompose the problem into 222 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 492. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
